ama_clear_candidate_effect = {
    remove_variable = ama_candidate
    remove_variable = ama_candidate_regiment
    remove_variable = ama_candidate_title
    set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_source value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_quote_source value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_best_score value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_best_deficit value = -1 }
    set_variable = { name = ama_best_size value = 999999 }
}

ama_find_title_expand_effect = {
    save_scope_as = ama_owner
    every_held_title = {
        limit = { ama_title_owned_trigger = yes }
        save_scope_as = ama_scanned_title
        every_owned_title_maa_regiment = {
            limit = { ama_title_expandable_trigger = yes }
            save_scope_as = ama_scanned_regiment
            save_scope_value_as = { name = ama_title_scanned_size value = maa_size }
            scope:ama_owner = {
                if = { limit = { scope:ama_title_scanned_size < var:ama_best_size }
                    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_skirmishers > 0 scope:ama_scanned_regiment = { is_maa_type = abudrar } }
                        set_variable = { name = ama_quote_source value = 1 }
                        ama_quote_1_effect = yes
                        if = { limit = { ama_affordable_trigger = yes }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate value = 1 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_mode value = 2 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_category value = 11 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_source value = 1 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_title value = scope:ama_scanned_title }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_regiment value = scope:ama_scanned_regiment }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_best_size value = scope:ama_title_scanned_size }
                        }
                    }
                    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_skirmishers > 0 scope:ama_scanned_regiment = { is_maa_type = accolade_maa_skirmishers } }
                        set_variable = { name = ama_quote_source value = 1 }
                        ama_quote_2_effect = yes
                        if = { limit = { ama_affordable_trigger = yes }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate value = 2 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_mode value = 2 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_category value = 11 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_source value = 1 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_title value = scope:ama_scanned_title }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_regiment value = scope:ama_scanned_regiment }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_best_size value = scope:ama_title_scanned_size }
                        }
                    }
                    if = { limit = { var:ama_target_skirmishers > 0 scope:ama_scanned_regiment = { is_maa_type = akritai } }
                        set_variable = { name = ama_quote_source value = 1 }
                        ama_quote_3_effect = yes
                        if = { limit = { ama_affordable_trigger = yes }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate value = 3 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_mode value = 2 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_category value = 11 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_source value = 1 }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_title value = scope:ama_scanned_title }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_candidate_regiment value = scope:ama_scanned_regiment }
                            set_variable = { name = ama_best_size value = scope:ama_title_scanned_size }
